            if tbl_49_upvr.fireAnim.IsPlaying then
                repeat
                    task.wait()
                until tbl_49_upvr.fireAnim.IsPlaying == false
            end
            if tbl_49_upvr.PumpAnim.IsPlaying then
                tbl_49_upvr.PumpAnim:Stop()
                tbl_49_upvr.tpMagCheckAnim:Stop()
            end
            print("tirando granada")
            tbl_49_upvr.PumpAnim:Stop()
            tbl_49_upvr.tpMagCheckAnim:Stop()
            tbl_49_upvr.fireAnim:Stop()
            tbl_49_upvr.tacticalReloadAnim:Play()
            tbl_49_upvr.tpEmptyReloadAnim:Play()
            var3705_upvw = false
            var3704_upvw = false
            repeat
                task.wait()
            until tbl_49_upvr.tacticalReloadAnim.IsPlaying == false
            local var3706 = tbl_7_upvr
            var3706.grenadesAMT -= 1
            print(tbl_7_upvr.grenadesAMT)
            HUD_upvr_2.Equipment.Frame.FlashBang.TextLabel.Text = 'x'..tbl_7_upvr.grenadesAMT
            if tbl_7_upvr.grenadesAMT <= 0 then
                print("No more grenades")
                tbl_7_upvr.currentSlot = 1
                loadslot(tbl_7_upvr.inventory[1])
            else
                loadslot(tbl_7_upvr.inventory[4])
            end
            any_7_upvw = false
            var2627_upvw = false
            print("granada tirada")
        end
    end

function interpolateRotation(arg1, arg2, arg3) -- Line 7408
    return CFrame.new(arg1.Position, arg1.Position + arg1 - arg1.Position:Lerp(arg2 - arg2.Position, arg3).LookVector)
end

function toggleGunTorch() -- Line 8733
    --[[ Upvalues[2]:
        [1]: tbl_7_upvr (readonly)
        [2]: Engine_upvr (readonly)
    ]]
    if tbl_7_upvr.viewmodel and tbl_7_upvr.viewmodel:FindFirstChild("FlashPoint") then
        if tbl_7_upvr.module.gunTorchOn == true then
            tbl_7_upvr.module.gunTorchOn = false
        else
            tbl_7_upvr.module.gunTorchOn = true
        end
        for _, v_142 in pairs(tbl_7_upvr.viewmodel:GetDescendants()) do
            if v_142:IsA("SpotLight") then
                v_142.Enabled = tbl_7_upvr.module.gunTorchOn
            end
        end
        if tbl_7_upvr.inventory[tbl_7_upvr.currentSlot] == "Ballistic shield" then
            for _, v_143 in pairs(Engine_upvr.Modules:GetDescendants()) do
                if v_143.Name == tbl_7_upvr.inventory[2] then
                    require(v_143).gunTorchOn = tbl_7_upvr.module.gunTorchOn
                end
            end
        end
        game.ReplicatedStorage.Engine.Events.serverSidedFlashLight:FireServer(tbl_7_upvr.inventory[tbl_7_upvr.currentSlot], tbl_7_upvr.module.gunTorchOn)
        print("light: ", tbl_7_upvr.module.gunTorchOn)
    end
end
